# AEO Master Guide — Consolidated v4 — 2026-11-17

- version: 4 (v3 = 2026-11-17 · v2 = 2026-11-16 · v1 = Claude Advanced Research, 2026-11-16) · built 2026-11-17 08:13 America/Phoenix
- scope: on-site answer engine optimization — featured snippets, PAA, AI Overviews, AI Mode, ChatGPT search, Perplexity, Copilot / Bing, Gemini, Claude, Meta AI, Siri, Alexa, voice; off-site findings parked
- inputs:
  - BASE: consolidated v3 — 2026-11-17 (labels inside: `CAR`, `PPLX`, `Unknown-2`); F-01–F-25, C1–C12, U1–U50, P-01–P-23
  - INCOMING D: "AEO Gap-Fill Research — Run A (G-01–G-11)", Claude research-agent pass 1 + web search/fetch pass 2, merged — 2026-11-17 01:00 (label `RUN-A`); NA-01–NA-46, policy table, token matrix
  - INCOMING E: "AEO Gap-Fill Research — Run B (G-12–G-22 + discovery)", Claude research mode pass 1 + pass 2 completion, merged, parts 1 + 2 — 2026-11-17 01:02 (label `RUN-B`); NB-01–NB-46
  - Earlier: `Unknown-1` co-hosting review — excluded in v2 (different subject)
- reference: `on-site-seo-geo-reference-unified-v4.6.md` (handbook) — `handbook:` tags on F- findings only
- verification: checked 2026-11-17 — 92 of 132 distinct RUN-A / RUN-B source URLs re-opened (114 of 155 source lines): 85 VERIFIED, 6 VERIFIED with a detail mismatch or denominator note, 1 PARTIAL (abstract only); none contradicted its finding's main claim. Re-opened v1–v3 sources: Search Engine Land RESONEO article, OtterlyAI schema experiment, Google AI-optimization guide, Bing preferences post. Sources not re-opened carry `RUN-A-opened` / `RUN-B-opened` or `excerpt only`. Never readable: Bing Webmaster Guidelines (JavaScript-only), Adweek (paywall).
- counts v4: RUN-A 129 items · RUN-B 86 items → findings 66 new, 23 overlap, 3 duplicate (same study), every block kept · policy rows 24 · token rows 31 (+2 added in v4) · off-site rows 18 · gap-status rows 22 · conflicts: 10 new (C13–C22), 3 closed (C4, C7, C8), 1 superseded (C5), 1 narrowed (C10) · unverified: 9 new (U51–U59), 1 resolved (U27), 4 partly resolved or sourced (U23, U25, U28, U39) · risk rows: 10 new
- totals: 117 findings (F 25 · NA 46 · NB 46) · 64 rules · 22 conflicts · 59 unverified claims · 23 parked items · 22 gaps (16 CLOSED, 6 PARTIAL)
- layout: Legend → Rules → Top findings → Claim-level grades → Findings by topic → reference tables → Conflicts → Gaps → Residual weaknesses → audit (Unverified claims, Verification log, Parked, Merge log, Prior versions)

## Legend

- grades (finding-level; as graded by the reporting run, kept unless v4 verification changed them): E1 controlled test or platform-owner document · E2 two or more independent studies or cases with outcome data · E3 one study or case, or correlational with disclosed sample · E4 claim without outcome data, opinion or secondary summary
- claim-level grade: grade after pooling independent studies across runs — table "Claim-level grades"
- risk: OK · GREY · VIOLATES (policy URL given)
- metrics, never merged: picked as snippet · cited with link · brand mentioned · facts stated correctly · clicks
- IDs: F-01–F-25 = v1–v3 findings (labels CAR, PPLX, Unknown-2) · NA-01–NA-46 = RUN-A (gaps G-01–G-11) · NB-01–NB-46 = RUN-B (gaps G-12–G-22 + discovery) · R = rule · G = gap · Q = targeted check · C = conflict · U = unverified claim · P = parked · handbook §x = `on-site-seo-geo-reference-unified-v4.6.md`
- existing IDs never renumbered; NA-/NB- IDs kept so cross-references inside the run reports still resolve
- fields: `app_check` (RUN-A / RUN-B field `check` renamed) · `fills` = gap IDs · `baseline` = relation to a v1–v3 finding · `pass2` = change made inside the run's second pass · `v4` = change or cross-reference added in this consolidation · `handbook` tag exists on F- findings only
- source tags: `VERIFIED <date>` = page re-opened that day and states the claim · `VERIFIED (detail MISMATCH)` = main claim holds, one detail differs (see the finding's v4 line and Unverified claims) · `PARTIAL` = only part of the claim visible in what was read · `CAR-run` / `RUN-A-opened` / `RUN-B-opened` = opened inside that research run, not re-opened in consolidation · `RUN-A: excerpt only` / `RUN-B: excerpt only` = the run itself used a search-result excerpt, snippet or paywalled lede; not re-opened · `UNREACHABLE` · `NO SOURCE`
- baseline map (gap-prompt B-IDs → finding IDs; shown inline as `F-xx [B-nn]`): B-01 F-01 · B-02 F-16 · B-03 F-02 · B-04 F-03 · B-05 F-04 (plus Google guide statements: no special schema, chunking, llms.txt or Markdown needed) · B-06 F-05 · B-07 F-06 · B-08 F-07 · B-09 F-08 · B-10 F-09 · B-11 F-10 · B-12 F-11 · B-13 F-12 · B-14 F-13 · B-15 F-14 · B-16 F-15 · B-17 F-17–F-19 · B-18 F-21–F-22 · B-19 platform-doc baseline (Q1, Q4; handbook §16, §18)

## Rules (by topic)

Each rule compresses the `do_this` lines of the findings cited. Grade = strongest evidence behind the rule. ⚠ = open conflict.

### 1 Access and crawler tokens

| R    | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Grade               | Evidence                                               |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| R-01 | Allow search and index bots in robots.txt and WAF: OAI-SearchBot, PerplexityBot, Claude-SearchBot, Applebot, Amzn-SearchBot, meta-webindexer, Bingbot, Googlebot; allowlist their published IP ranges                                                                                                              | E1                  | NA-17, NA-21, NA-18, NA-23, NA-10, NA-09, Token matrix |
| R-02 | Allow user fetchers that honor robots.txt (Claude-User); expect ChatGPT-User, Perplexity-User, Google-Agent, Meta-ExternalFetcher and Amzn-User to ignore it — block those by IP or WAF if a block is needed                                                                                                       | E1                  | NA-17, NA-18, NA-20, NA-21, NA-09, NA-10               |
| R-03 | Decide training tokens separately (GPTBot, ClaudeBot, meta-externalagent, Applebot-Extended, MistralAI-Training, Amazonbot, `__usage_ai-training`); OpenAI, Anthropic, Apple and Bing document them as separate from search inclusion; Google will read `__usage_ai-training` as a Google-Extended disallow (R-04) | E1                  | NA-17, NA-18, NA-09, NA-23, NA-19, NA-08, NB-18        |
| R-04 | Keep Google-Extended allowed on pages meant for Gemini grounding; it does not control AIO or AI Mode. If `__usage_ai-training` is disallowed, add an explicit Google-Extended group                                                                                                                                | E1                  | NA-24, NB-18, C7                                       |
| R-05 | To keep a page out of ChatGPT search use `noindex` with the crawler allowed; never combine `noindex` with a robots.txt block                                                                                                                                                                                       | E1                  | NA-26                                                  |
| R-06 | Every named robots.txt group repeats the full Disallow set; a group holding only `Allow: /` cancels the `*` rules                                                                                                                                                                                                  | SPEC (handbook §18) | C9                                                     |
| R-07 | Get answer-worthy URLs indexed in Google, Bing (IndexNow) and Brave; indexation, fetch, citation and accuracy are separate outcomes                                                                                                                                                                                | E3                  | NB-02, NB-20, NB-23, F-03, F-10, F-19                  |
| R-08 | Keep target pages indexable and snippet-eligible; add no AI-specific files or markup for Google                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | E1                  | NA-25, NB-40                                           |

### 2 Snippet and AI-use controls

| R    | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Grade | Evidence            |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ------------------- |
| R-09 | Never ship `nosnippet` or `max-snippet:0` on pages meant for featured snippets, AIO or AI Mode; no `nosnippet` on content meant for Siri answers; use `max-snippet:-1`                                   | E1    | NA-01, NA-02, NB-33 |
| R-10 | Use `data-nosnippet` only on passages to exclude (Bing snippets and Copilot answers; Google snippets); never on the answer block                                                                         | E1    | NA-04               |
| R-11 | Bing: no NOARCHIVE or NOCACHE on Copilot target pages; set the page-level AI Grounding preference explicitly — options open 2026-12-15, effective 2027-02-01, legacy tags stop counting after 2027-03-01 | E1    | F-02, NA-08         |
| R-12 | Google AIO / AI Mode exclusion today = property-level Search generative AI control or `nosnippet`; page-level control due 2027-03-03 (news reports only)                                                 | E4    | NB-45               |
| R-13 | If Cloudflare Markdown for Agents is enabled, set your own Content-Signal header; the default grants AI training                                                                                         | E1    | NB-19               |

### 3 Rendering and delivery

| R    | Rule                                                                                                                              | Grade                        | Evidence                  |
| ---- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------- |
| R-14 | Put every answer-critical fact (price, spec, date, hours, address, phone) in visible server HTML; none JS-only, none JSON-LD-only | E2 (claim-level) ⚠ C15, C18 | F-09, NB-10, NB-11, NA-12 |
| R-15 | Keep raw HTML under 4 MB; audit CSS-hidden text blocks over 100 words — the ChatGPT cache extracts hidden text                    | E3                           | F-09                      |
| R-16 | Same facts in raw HTML and rendered DOM; same main content for every user agent                                                   | E3                           | NB-11, NB-34, F-15        |
| R-17 | Put the core answer sentence directly after the H1; move labels, dates, bylines, TOC below it                                     | E3                           | F-08, NA-29               |

### 4 Retrieval and selection

| R    | Rule                                                                                                                                             | Grade                   | Evidence                        |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------- | ------------------------------- |
| R-18 | Do not treat Google top-10 rank or Bing rank as a proxy for AI citation; track citations per surface and per ChatGPT tier                        | E2 (claim-level) ⚠ C19 | F-24, NB-25, NB-06, F-10, NB-20 |
| R-19 | Answer fan-out sub-questions on one page per user need; title restates the specific question; never one page per query variation                 | E3 / E1 (policy)        | NB-46, NB-25, F-04              |
| R-20 | Bing / Copilot: single-topic, self-contained pages; current, non-contradictory facts; IndexNow on every substantive change                       | E1                      | F-03, NA-27                     |
| R-21 | Prioritize query relevance, explicit prices and dates over formatting emphasis                                                                   | E1 (lab)                | F-06, NA-16                     |
| R-22 | New pages: judge citation at day 30; review Perplexity and AI Mode in week 1, ChatGPT and Copilot in week 2, Gemini, AIO and Claude in weeks 3–4 | E3                      | NB-21, NB-22, NB-01             |
| R-23 | Claude: check Brave Search indexation and rank before expecting citations                                                                        | E3                      | NB-23                           |

### 5 Content structure

| R    | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                         | Grade              | Evidence                   |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------ | -------------------------- |
| R-24 | Answer the main question in the first sentence under the H1 and under each H2                                                                                                                | E3 (correlational) | NA-29, F-08                |
| R-25 | Phrase H2 / H3 as the user's question where the section answers one                                                                                                                          | E3 (correlational) | NA-31                      |
| R-26 | Comparisons in a real `<table>`, steps in `<ol>`, prices as visible text; FAQ section only where real questions exist; include definitions, numbers, comparisons, steps as extractable units | E3 (correlational) | NA-30, NA-32, F-06         |
| R-27 | Keep a clear heading hierarchy; expect nothing from bolding or emphasis alone                                                                                                                | E1 (lab) / E3      | F-06, F-13                 |
| R-28 | 40–60-word answer blocks and key-facts blocks: no evidence either way; keep as usability practice only                                                                                       | none               | NA-31, Q2                  |
| R-29 | Featured snippets are shrinking and PAA answers are mostly AI-generated: treat PAA as an AIO citation surface; measure feature presence per query and per market                             | E3 ⚠ C16          | NA-37, NA-38, NA-39, NA-03 |

| R    | Rule                                                                                        | Grade         | Evidence                         |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------- |

| R-33 | Do not rely on VideoObject `transcript`; use Clip or SeekToAction for key moments           | E1            | NA-35                            |

| ---- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ----------------- |

| R-36 | Do not expect freshness alone to move AI Overviews                                                                                | E3 ⚠ C12                       | F-23              |

### 9 Brand facts and corrections

| R    | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                        | Grade | Evidence           |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ------------------ |
| R-38 | For each wrong AI claim, publish one crawlable owned page stating the correct fact in a plain, dated sentence; state services offered and not offered, locations and comparisons explicitly | E2    | NB-03, F-17, NB-24 |
| R-39 | Keep one HTML brand-facts page, internally linked, refreshed on a schedule; confirm AI user agents fetch it before measuring                                                                | E3    | NB-04, NB-05       |
| R-40 | A correction is a source, not an override: verify each fact on every target surface; include false-premise prompts in checks                                                                | E3    | F-18, F-20         |
| R-41 | No contradictory values for one fact across your own pages                                                                                                                                  | E1    | NA-27, F-17        |

### 16 Never (policy and risk)

| R    | Rule                                                                                                        | Risk                               | Grade | Evidence                  |
| ---- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ----- | ------------------------- |
| R-58 | No pages per fan-out or query variation                                                                     | VIOLATES (Google)                  | E1    | F-04, NA-05               |

| R-60 | No markup of content not visible on the page; no fake FAQs                                                  | VIOLATES (Google)                  | E1    | NA-45                     |
| R-61 | No pseudo-independent sites, fake reviews or personas corroborating your own claims                         | VIOLATES (Anthropic, Claude users) | E1    | NA-42                     |
| R-62 | No content variation by user agent for AI crawlers                                                          | VIOLATES (inferred) / GREY         | E4    | F-15, NB-34               |
| R-63 | No self-ranked "best X" listicles; publish comparisons with disclosed criteria                              | GREY                               | E3    | F-12, NA-07, NA-44        |
| R-64 | No date-only or year-in-title bumps                                                                         | GREY                               | E3    | F-07, NA-34               |

## Claim-level grades (cross-study)

Agreement between reports is not evidence; independence is judged by underlying study.

| Claim                                                              | Claim grade        | Independent evidence                                                                                 | Caveat                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Adding schema yields no measurable AI-citation lift                | E1                 | F-01 (Ahrefs, controlled); corroborating: NA-46 (Otterly), NB-38 (Insites)                           | Contrary correlational: NA-30 (+13% odds). No type-isolated test. C1, C11      |
| ChatGPT, Claude and Perplexity fetchers do not execute JavaScript  | E2                 | F-09 (RESONEO), NB-10 (Forge logs), NB-11 (Search Engine World; RESONEO Jan 2026)                    | One contrary Perplexity test; Copilot and Grok inconsistent. C18               |
| Facts held only in JSON-LD are not reliably read                   | E2                 | F-09 (cache strips JSON-LD), NA-12 (searchVIU; Otterly)                                              | One test saw invalid JSON-LD read as page text. C15                            |
| Single-run AI visibility checks are unreliable                     | E2                 | F-11 (EWR; St. Gallen; SparkToro), NA-15 (Ahrefs), NA-41 (TJ Digital; Profound)                      | Run count unsettled. C2                                                        |
| Google top-10 rank is a weak proxy for AIO citation                | E2                 | F-24 / NB-25 (Ahrefs), NB-06 (On-Page.ai, 50 keywords)                                               | Method change between Ahrefs studies                                           |
| ChatGPT search leans on Google results, most on paid tiers         | E2                 | F-10 (RESONEO), NB-02 (Solís; Backlinko sting), NB-20 (AirOps; Grow and Convert)                     | Magnitude 16.61%–≈75% by method and tier. C5, C19                              |
| AI-cited pages skew recently updated (ChatGPT, Gemini, Perplexity) | E2 (correlational) | F-23 / NA-14 / NB-28 (Ahrefs, one study), NA-33 (Seer), NA-33 (TJ Digital)                           | Date signal never isolated; AIO differs. C12                                   |
| llms.txt does not drive citations                                  | E2                 | F-25 (SE Ranking), NB-35 (Ahrefs logs), NB-12 (AnswerShare; dev.to log), NB-05 source (Seer, 0 of 3) | One five-pair controlled positive on Perplexity. C14                           |
| An owned page stating the correct fact changes wrong AI answers    | E2                 | NB-03 (Seer; Verticality), F-17 (Ahrefs experiment), NB-05 (Seer client tests)                       | No controls; effect decays (NB-04); not universal (F-18)                       |
| Direct answers, tables and lists correlate with citation           | E2 (correlational) | NA-29 (TJ Digital; Indig), NA-30 (TJ Digital; AirOps), NA-32 (arXiv), F-13 (GEO-SFE, lab)            | Lab: formatting-only edits little impact (F-06). No controlled production test |
| AIO presence lowers organic click-through; citation recovers part  | E2                 | NB-31 (Ahrefs), NB-09 (Seer)                                                                         | Desktop / informational queries; correlational                                 |
| Local AI answers cite the business's own website heavily           | E2                 | NB-38 (Insites), NB-15 (BrightLocal), NB-39 (Steady Demand)                                          | On-site factors not isolated                                                   |
| Bylines alone do not lift AIO citations                            | E1 (null)          | NB-17 (Seer, controlled); NB-43 (DAP)                                                                | Bing and practitioner lifts conflict. C22                                      |
| Self-ranked listicles lose the brand recommendation in AIO         | E3                 | F-12, NA-07 (same author, two waves); NA-44 (Peec, different metric)                                 | Not independently replicated on the same metric                                |

## Findings (by topic)

Blocks are verbatim from their source report except: `check` renamed `app_check`, baseline IDs shown as `F-xx [B-nn]`, `reported_by` lines and source tags added. Other added lines are marked `v4:`. One block per finding; cross-topic links sit in the v4 lines and the Rules.

### T03 Rendering, JavaScript, page delivery

#### F-09 ChatGPT cache: no JSON-LD, no JS, 4 MB cap

what: ChatGPT reading cache converts HTML to Markdown, strips scripts/JSON-LD, extracts CSS-hidden text, executes no JS, rejects pages >4 MB with HTTP 400; copies "fresh for about 30 minutes", some served >90 days later; no-store and noindex ignored.
surfaces: ChatGPT | metric: facts stated correctly; cited with link | grade: E3 | risk: OK
handbook: changes §14, §24
⚠ CONFLICT (C10): Unknown-2 cites Honeybot Observatory — ~1.5% of ~1.4M eligible responses executed JS; OAI-SearchBot ~27% and GPTBot ~6.8% of attributed executions (NO SOURCE). Different fetchers (index crawler vs reading cache) may explain both.
do_this: "Keep every AI-critical fact in visible server HTML; keep HTML under 4 MB; never rely on JSON-LD for facts."
app_check: "Flag raw HTML >4 MB; flag facts present only in JSON-LD; flag CSS-hidden text blocks >100 words."
v4: "no JS" replicated independently — NB-10 (server logs), NB-11 (12-assistant test; RESONEO January 2026); claim-level grade E2. JSON-LD not read as schema — NA-12, NB-11 pass2 line. C10 narrowed; C15, C18 new.
reported_by: CAR

### T06 Structured data

#### F-01 Adding schema does not lift AI citations

what: Ahrefs matched difference-in-differences: 1,885 pages adding JSON-LD (Aug 2025–Mar 2026) vs 4,000 controls; AIO −4.6% (significant), AI Mode +2.4%, ChatGPT +2.2% (both noise).

[PPLX + source re-check]
surfaces: AIO, AI Mode, ChatGPT | metric: cited with link | grade: E1 | risk: OK
handbook: confirms §24, §16 (CAR) · ⚠ CONFLICT: PPLX tags "changes §24" — Conflicts C1

do_this: ⚠ CONFLICT (C1): CAR "Do not add or expand JSON-LD to gain AI citations; keep schema only for rich-result eligibility." vs PPLX "Implement structured data for documented features and accurate entity descriptions; do not promise additional AI citations from JSON-LD alone."
app_check: CAR "Report any AI-citation uplift claim tied to schema as unsupported; no site threshold." | not detectable from the site · PPLX "Assign zero evidence-backed citation-uplift points solely for JSON-LD presence; validate supported features and visible-content consistency separately."
v4: corroborated — NA-46 (site-wide schema rollout: no attributable lift; same experiment as source 2 below), NB-38 (local: structured data shows no relationship), NA-12 (JSON-LD-only facts not reported in 2 of 3 tests). Contrary, correlational — NA-30 (schema +13% citation odds). Platform positions — NA-11 (Bing: aids understanding), NB-40 (Google: not required). C1, C11 updated.

### T07 Freshness and dates

#### F-05 LLM rerankers favor newer-dated passages

what: Fang et al.: injecting artificial dates into TREC DL21/22 passages shifted Top-10 mean year up to 4.78 years, moved items up to 95 ranks; pairwise preferences reversed up to 25%.
surfaces: LLM rerankers (GPT-3.5/4/4o, LLaMA-3, Qwen-2.5; lab) | metric: picked as source (ranking) | grade: E1 | risk: OK
handbook: changes §28
do_this: "Show a visible, accurate last-updated date on maintained pages; production weighting per surface remains undocumented."
app_check: "Flag article pages with no visible machine-readable date (time element or dateModified)."
v4: production direction, correlational — F-23 / NA-14, NA-33. No production test isolates the date signal (RUN-A G-06).
reported_by: CAR
sources:

- Study | arXiv (Waseda University) | Do Large Language Models Favor Recent Content? A Study on Recency Bias in LLM-Based Reranking | https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11353 | 2026-09-21 | CAR-run

#### F-23 Assistants cite fresher pages; AI Overviews do not

what: Ahrefs Brand Radar, ~16.975M cited URLs: assistant-cited pages average 1,064 days since publication vs 1,432 for organic SERPs ("25.7% fresher"); update age 909 vs 1,047 days (13.1%). AIO top-3 citations are 16 days older than organic; ChatGPT citations 458 days newer; Perplexity and ChatGPT in-text references ordered newest first.
limits: Correlational; no time window stated; average cited page still 2.9 years old; table and text disagree on two figures (AIO update age 1,067 vs 1,047; organic publication age 1,416 vs 1,432). Author: "Google is the least influenced by content freshness."
surfaces: ChatGPT, Perplexity, Gemini, Copilot, AIO | metric: cited with link | grade: E3 | risk: OK
handbook: changes §16 ("Currency: AI Overviews favor freshly updated content" — unsourced; Conflicts C12), changes §28
⚠ CONFLICT (C6): Unknown-2 cites "82% of Perplexity citations <30 days old" (Leapd, NO SOURCE) vs Perplexity in-text cited pages averaging 1,166 days since publication here.
do_this: "Refresh content substantively to compete on ChatGPT, Perplexity, Gemini and Copilot; do not expect freshness alone to move AI Overviews; never change dates without a content change."
app_check: "Flag a visible date or dateModified change with no body-text change since the previous crawl (see F-07)."
v4: same study reported by RUN-A (NA-14) and RUN-B (NB-28) — one source, three reports. Independent support for update-date freshness: NA-33 (Seer; TJ Digital). AIO exception contested by TJ Digital's figure (updated ≤12 months +88% odds; study covers ChatGPT, AIO and AI Mode, engine split not captured) — C12. No original found for the Perplexity "<30 days" claims (NB-28) — C6.
reported_by: Unknown-2 (per-platform figures and AIO exception added from source)
sources:

- Vendor | Ahrefs (Ryan Law; contributor Xibeijia Guan) | title not captured | https://ahrefs.com/blog/do-ai-assistants-prefer-to-cite-fresh-content/ | 2025-07-28 (modified 2026-04-27) | VERIFIED 2026-11-17

#### NA-14 AI-cited pages skew fresher; AIO does not

what: AI-cited URLs 25.7% fresher by publish date, 13.1% by last update vs organic; AIO cites 16 days older; ChatGPT cites 458 days newer than organic.
fills: G-06 | baseline: replicates F-05 [B-06] direction in production (correlational)
surfaces: ChatGPT search, Perplexity, Gemini app, Microsoft Copilot / Bing, AIO | metric: cited with link | grade: E3 (correlational) | risk: OK
do_this: "Refresh substantive content on ChatGPT/Perplexity-targeted pages; do not rely on freshness for AIO."
app_check: "Flag cited-target pages with last real content change >13 months."
limits: Correlational; 16.975M cited URLs; date-only changes not isolated. Pass-1 figures: average cited page 1,064 days vs 1,432 organic; 909-day figure also reported — not re-verified.
v4: duplicate of F-23 (same Ahrefs study). The "1,064 vs 909 days" question in limits is settled by F-23: 1,064 = days since publication, 909 = days since last update.
reported_by: RUN-A
sources:

#### NA-33 Freshness tracks last update, not publish date

fills: G-06 | baseline: replicates F-05 [B-06] direction (independent of NA-14; correlational)

#### NA-34 Dates must reflect real publish or update; title year correlates

fills: G-06, G-11 | baseline: none (F-07 [B-08] not replicated; year-in-title is adjacent and correlational)
surfaces: Google Search byline dates, ChatGPT search, AIO, AI Mode | metric: cited with link | grade: E1 (doc) / E3 (TJ, correlational) | risk: GREY (date or year change without content change)

### T08 llms.txt, Markdown, content signals

#### F-25 llms.txt shows no link to AI citations

what: SE Ranking, ~300,000 domains: 10.13% had llms.txt (8.27% for 100,001+ visit sites); Spearman, XGBoost and SHAP found no correlation with citation frequency across sampled LLM responses; the model was more accurate with llms.txt removed.
limits: Correlational; LLMs, response count and time window not stated; no coefficients or accuracy metrics published; author calls results context-dependent.
surfaces: unnamed LLMs | metric: cited with link | grade: E3 | risk: OK
handbook: confirms §20, §23 (adds non-Google evidence)
do_this: "Do not build llms.txt expecting AI citations; build it only when a named consumer documents reading it."
app_check: "Report llms.txt presence as neutral; award no citation points for it."
v4: independent support — NB-35 (Ahrefs: 97% of llms.txt files never requested), NB-12 (no Markdown requests from frontier crawlers), NB-05 source (Seer: llms.txt adopted in 0 of 3 tests; stopped recommending it), NB-38 (local: no relationship). Contrary — NB-36 (five-pair controlled test: Perplexity +10.2 points). C14.
reported_by: Unknown-2 ("November 2025" date = MISMATCH U49)
sources:

- Vendor (SEO tool; commercial conflict) | SE Ranking (Yulia Deda) | Does LLMs.txt impact your AI visibility and citations? No, according to research | https://seranking.com/blog/llms-txt/ | 2026-10-15 (page metadata; possibly a republish) | VERIFIED 2026-11-17
- News | Search Engine Journal | LLMs.txt Shows No Clear Effect On AI Citations, Based On 300k Domains | https://www.searchenginejournal.com/llms-txt-shows-no-clear-effect-on-ai-citations-based-on-300k-domains/561542/ | date not captured | search-result text only

#### NB-35 97% of llms.txt files are never fetched

what: 137,210 domains: 28% publish llms.txt; 97% of those files got zero requests in May 2026; AI retrieval bots made 1.1% of requests.
fills: G-18 | baseline: replicates F-04 [B-05] (independent)
surfaces: ChatGPT search, Perplexity, Claude | metric: cited with link | grade: E3 | risk: OK
do_this: "Skip llms.txt for answer-surface citations; keep it only if AI coding agents are an audience."
app_check: "Zero /llms.txt requests from OAI-SearchBot, ChatGPT-User, PerplexityBot, Claude-SearchBot, Claude-User over 30 days = no-op asset."
limits: Fetch is not use; one month; UA-based classification; customer base skews technical; coding agents made 10.5% of requests.
v4: the 10.5% covers the "AI agents & agentic infrastructure" category (Claude-Code is one example), not coding agents alone → U58; the page describes shares both as % of total requests and % of AI bot requests.
reported_by: RUN-B
sources:

- Vendor | Ahrefs (Louise Linehan) | We Analyzed 137K Sites: 97% of llms.txt Files Never Get Read | https://ahrefs.com/blog/llmstxt-study/ | 2026-06-15 | 137,210 domains, May 2026, bot analytics; AI assistants (ChatGPT-User, Claude-User) 2.5% of requests; training crawlers 5.3%; no AI bot requested a missing llms.txt | VERIFIED 2026-11-17 (detail MISMATCH)

#### NB-12 Frontier crawlers never request Markdown

what: Frontier AI crawlers sent Accept: text/markdown 0 times in 1,277,965 requests; Cloudflare serves Markdown only on explicit request.
fills: G-18 | baseline: none
surfaces: ChatGPT search, Claude, Perplexity | metric: cited with link | grade: E3 | risk: OK

app_check: "Zero /llms.txt or text/markdown requests from AI UAs over 30 days = no-op asset."
limits: Five properties; vendor with competing product; measures delivery, not citations.
pass2: Cloudflare doc opened: conversion happens only when the client sends Accept: text/markdown; doc names no crawler that does. Larger log study → NB-35. Controlled test → NB-36. Google's statement that Search does not use llms.txt or Markdown files = F-04 [B-05] (same source, not re-reported).
v4: AnswerShare page — published 2026-08-06 (not 2026-08-17); 1,258,190 of the 1,277,965 events had saved headers and none requested text/markdown; 19,775 had no saved header (missing, not zero) → U52.

sources:

- Practitioner | DEV Community (Asad Abdullah Zafar) | llms.txt: 60 Days of Server Logs, Zero AI Crawler Requests | https://dev.to/asad_abdullah_zafar/llmstxt-60-days-of-server-logs-zero-ai-crawler-requests-3ld2 | 2026 | 4,218 AI crawler requests, 60 days, 0 to /llms.txt | RUN-B-opened

### T09 Brand facts and corrections

#### F-17 Official correction FAQ steered ChatGPT

what: Ahrefs fictional brand (xarumei.com), 56 questions, 8 products: after an official FAQ plus three seeded fake sources (blog, Reddit AMA, Medium), ChatGPT-4 and ChatGPT-5 Thinking resisted, cited the FAQ in 84% of answers, stayed under 7% misinformation.
limits: One synthetic brand; FAQ and fake sources introduced together (FAQ not isolated); products tested via API except AI Mode; no collection dates (testing ran "two months"); model versions since superseded.
surfaces: ChatGPT (ChatGPT-4, ChatGPT-5 Thinking, API) | metric: facts stated correctly; cited with link | grade: E3 | risk: OK
handbook: confirms §6, §25
do_this: "Publish concise, visible corrections for consequential false claims; state the true fact and explicitly reject the misleading alternative."
app_check: "Flag contradictory founder, location, product, ownership, and pricing statements across live pages; require human verification before publishing corrections."
v4: NB-03 (two agency cases, E2), NB-05 (HTML facts page adopted in 3 of 4 tests), NB-04 (effect decays without refresh).
reported_by: PPLX (84% and <7% added from source)
sources:

- Vendor | Ahrefs (Mateusz Makosiewicz) | I Ran an AI Misinformation Experiment. Every Marketer Should See the Results (title per PPLX) | https://ahrefs.com/blog/ai-vs-made-up-brand-experiment/ | 2025-12-10 | VERIFIED 2026-11-16

surfaces: Perplexity, Copilot, Gemini, AI Mode, Grok | metric: facts stated correctly | grade: E3 | risk: OK
handbook: changes §16, §25
do_this: "Treat the official facts page as a correction source, not an override; verify consequential facts independently on every target surface."
app_check: not detectable from the site
v4: NB-24 (inaccurate brand answers by surface: AIO 5.7%, Gemini 4.3%, Perplexity 3.3%, AI Mode 1.7%, ChatGPT 1.3%, Claude 1.1%).

app_check: not detectable from the site
v4: NB-24 (comparative prompts: 80.3% no answer).
reported_by: PPLX (Grok, Xiaomi detail added from source)

### T15 Measurement and reporting

#### F-11 Citation checks need far more than two runs

what: EWR v2 (28,752 answers, 5 engines, 2026-01-13→10-15): ChatGPT median cited-domain overlap 0.143; one run ±37 points; ±10 points needs 14 runs, 58 where brand appears intermittently. St. Gallen preprint (4 engines, 4 German-language campaigns × 8 prompts, up to 10 runs, 2026-03-21→25): within-24h source-set Jaccard 0.321–0.434; recommends ≥7 runs/prompt/day for brand visibility, ≥8 for source coverage (criterion: SE of per-brand detection rate < 0.10).
⚠ CONFLICT (C2): run count — CAR/EWR ≥14 (±10 points; 58 intermittent) vs PPLX/St. Gallen ≥7 brand / ≥8 source (SE < 0.10). Different precision targets; all three studies reject single-run checks and the handbook's 2 runs.
limits (St. Gallen): German prompts, Swiss IPs and locale; AIO excluded; zero-citation runs excluded from source analysis; author affiliated with Aurora Intelligence.
surfaces: ChatGPT, Perplexity, Gemini, Google AI Mode, Claude, DeepSeek | metric: cited with link; brand mentioned | grade: E3 (3 independent studies concur; none controlled) | risk: OK
handbook: changes §27
do_this: CAR "Run each panel prompt ≥14 times per engine per period; report presence rate with confidence interval, never single-run rank." · PPLX "Use repeated, fixed-configuration citation checks; treat eight runs per prompt as a study-specific starting point, not a reliability guarantee."
app_check: CAR "Flag AI-panel configs with <14 runs per prompt-engine pair." | not detectable from the site
v4: NA-15 (next-day citation loss 50–83%; judge over 7 days), NA-40 (SparkToro: 60–100 runs, heuristic — same study as the Search Engine Journal source below), NA-41 (repeat rates 23–28%). Claim-level grade E2 for "single runs unreliable"; run count still unsettled — C2 widened.
reported_by: CAR, PPLX; Unknown-2 (repeated runs, 20–50 fixed prompts, one variable changed at a time; no single-score or "#1" claims — NO SOURCE; its "32–43% identical sources in 24 h" = U20 MISMATCH)
sources:

- Vendor | EWR Digital | AI Answer Variance Study, Version 2: 28,752 Answers, Five Engines | https://www.ewrdigital.com/ai-answer-variance | 2026-11-05 | CAR-run
- Study | arXiv (EUAS / Rankfor.AI) | Where Does the Noise Come From? A Variance-Components Decomposition of Non-Determinism in LLM Brand Answers (12,933 responses; repeat past 5th cuts relative-error variance by 0.0003; spread across models/languages instead) | https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13304 | 2026-07 | CAR-run
- News | Search Engine Journal | AI Recommendations Change With Nearly Every Query: SparkToro (<1% identical brand lists) | https://www.searchenginejournal.com/ai-recommendations-change-with-nearly-every-query-sparktoro/566242/ | 2026-01-30 | CAR-run
- Study (commercial affiliation disclosed: Aurora Intelligence) | University of St. Gallen (Schulte, Bleeker, Kaufmann) | Don't Measure Once: Measuring Visibility in AI Search (GEO) | https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07585 | 2026-04-08 | VERIFIED 2026-11-16 (PPLX date 2026-04-10 = MISMATCH, U1)

### T16 Manipulation, policy, risk

#### F-04 Fan-out query page farms violate Google policy

what: Google guide: creating separate content for query variations or fan-out queries "primarily to manipulate rankings or generative AI responses" violates scaled content abuse policy; semantic HTML optional.
surfaces: AIO, AI Mode | metric: picked as snippet; cited with link | grade: E1 | risk: VIOLATES (https://developers.google.com/search/docs/fundamentals/ai-optimization-guide) · ⚠ PPLX rated "Scaled Q&A pages" GREY — Conflicts C4
handbook: changes §29
do_this: "Never generate pages per fan-out or query variation; consolidate into one page per user need."
app_check: "Flag ≥10 templated pages sharing >80% text and differing mainly in the query phrase."
v4: guide re-opened 2026-11-17 (last updated 2026-07-10) — statement confirmed. NA-05: Google's spam definition covers attempts to manipulate generative AI responses. C4 closed → VIOLATES.
reported_by: CAR (PPLX: related risk row, no source)
sources:

- Official | Google Search Central | Optimizing your website for generative AI features on Google Search | https://developers.google.com/search/docs/fundamentals/ai-optimization-guide.md.txt | 2026-05-15 | VERIFIED 2026-11-17 (v4; was CAR-run)

- searches with no result: controlled production test of answer-first / question headings / short answer blocks / key-facts blocks; featured-snippet selection test (controlled or before/after) since 2025; OpenAI or Perplexity site-owner manipulation policy; xAI official crawler doc; DeepSeek official crawler doc; consumer Gemini Live source-path documentation; controlled on-page transcript test; caption/transcript statement for AIO, ChatGPT, Perplexity, Copilot; JSON-LD statement from OpenAI, Perplexity, Anthropic, Apple, Amazon; independent replication of F-07 [B-08] (date-only bump); study isolating visible date vs `dateModified` vs `lastmod`; type-isolated schema citation test; `nosnippet` / `max-snippet` behavior in engines other than Google and Bing
- inaccessible sources: https://www.bing.com/webmasters/help/webmaster-guidelines-30fba23a — JavaScript-only in both passes (NA-06, NA-11 wording unverified); https://dailycoloradonews.com/ai-search-optimization-five-assumptions-checked-primary-sources-daily_colorado/ — site blocked (primary behind the ground.news lead); GEO paper Section 6 — only abstract opened (https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.09735), abstract has no deployed-engine numbers; Growth Memo original post (Indig) — not opened, cited via Search Engine Land; Google 2019 blog on byline dates ('artificially freshen' wording) — not opened; OpenAI Usage Policies — not opened
- opened, excluded under §5 (figures kept here for zero loss, not used as evidence):
  - https://www.searchpilot.com/resources/case-studies/top-brands-faqs — 2022-10-05, pre-2025; controlled split test, FAQ question added to category pages, organic sessions +9%; no AI metric
  - https://patrickstox.com/technical-seo/on-page/meta-tags/max-snippet-max-image-preview/ — page labeled AI-generated; gives no minimum `max-snippet` value; references a Glenn Gabe `nosnippet` test without figures
  - https://www.airops.com/report/the-2026-state-of-ai-search — 2025-12-02; no sample or method; claims: 30% of brands visible across back-to-back answers, 20% across five runs; pages not updated for 3+ months over 3× likelier to lose citations; sequential headings 2.8×; 61% of cited pages use 3+ schema types (13% higher likelihood); FAQ schema on 10.5% of cited pages; ~60% of AIO citations outside top 20
  - https://brightedge.com/resources/weekly-ai-search-insights/youtube-presence-ai-search — 2025-09-25; sample undisclosed; YouTube citation share AIO 29.5%, AI Mode 16.6%, Perplexity 9.7%, ChatGPT 0.2%; Vimeo 0.1%, TikTok 0.1%
  - https://ahrefs.com/blog/on-page-aeo/ — 2026-05-08; compiles third-party correlational figures; no original test
  - https://ahrefs.com/blog/fresh-content/ — 2025-12-22; restates the NA-14 study; no date-only test

  - https://ground.news/article/the-paper-everyone-cites-for-ai-search-tested-its-own-advice-on-a-real-engine-the-tactic-lost-three-quarters-of-its-effect — aggregator summary: GEO paper's deployed-engine gain for adding statistics 8.7% vs 30–40% headline; engine and n not stated; primary not opened — UNVERIFIED, and the paper is pre-2025 and not independent

Claude and Gemini (NB-22); Google AIPREF post opened, token mapping confirmed (NB-18); Cloudflare doc opened (NB-19); AI Mode shopping official statement (NB-40); Siri data source and controls (NB-33)

- assumption: search-result text from an official page that blocked fetching is treated as opened when quoted verbatim in results. (pass 1)
- assumption: Seer Interactive and Verticality count as independent cases for E2. (pass 1)
- assumption: G-16 "Closes when" met by one disclosed-n study for AIO only. (pass 1)
- assumption: pass 2 also treats a search-result excerpt of the original page as opened; each such source is labeled "(search-result excerpt of original)".
- assumption: status CLOSED = the gap's "Closes when" criterion is met; sub-questions with no evidence are listed under residual sub-questions rather than holding the gap at PARTIAL.
- assumption: small controlled tests keep grade E1 per §4 (control group present); weakness is stated in limits (NB-17, NB-36).
- assumption: `pass2:` lines record every change made to a pass-1 finding (zero-loss merge); findings NB-20 to NB-46 are new in pass 2.
- assumption: where pass 1 gave both a quoted check rule and "not detectable from the site", pass 2 keeps one form: NB-01, NB-02 keep "not detectable" and preserve the rule in the pass2 line; NB-03, NB-04 keep the quoted rule (site-detectable).

## Residual weaknesses

- SUPPORTED: evidence is mostly vendor and agency studies, correlational (E3). Controlled production tests are few: schema (F-01), bylines (NB-17), llms.txt (NB-36, five site pairs) and a 10-article date-bump test with 2 controls (F-07).
- SUPPORTED: six gaps remain PARTIAL — G-02, G-03, G-04, G-07, G-10, G-11. The "residual sub-questions" list under Gaps has no public evidence after two passes.
- SUPPORTED: v4 re-opened 92 of 132 distinct RUN-A / RUN-B source URLs; the rest carry run-opened tags only. Bing Webmaster Guidelines were never read directly (JavaScript-only page) — NA-06, NA-11, F-02, F-03 wording rests on snippets and news reports.
- SUPPORTED: v4 verification read each page through an automated fetch-and-answer step in 100,000-character windows; a YES can miss context. Per-language figures in NB-13 sit in a PDF that was not re-read.
- SUPPORTED: NA- / NB- findings carry no handbook § tag (the gap prompts had no reference field). Only handbook conflicts C7, C8, C9, C12 were re-assessed.
- SUPPORTED: several findings share one study (see Claim-level grades); three reports citing one study = one source.
- SUPPORTED: platform behavior is dated. Fastest-moving items: ChatGPT source mix (changed 2026-08-20, Off-site table), Bing AI preferences (effective 2027-02-01), Google page-level AI control (due 2027-03-03), shared AI-training token (2027).
- SPECULATIVE: claim-level E2 grades assume the pooled studies are independent; several rely on the same tracking vendors (Peec AI, Profound, Semrush).
- SPECULATIVE: heuristic thresholds — `max-snippet` 300 (NA-02), 30% question headings (NA-31), 150-word transcript (NA-35), 10 "best" URLs (NA-44), ≥50% title–query overlap used as a rule (NB-46), 90-day facts-page refresh (NB-04), 13-month staleness flag (NA-14).
- SPECULATIVE: transfer of API-based results to consumer apps — NB-23 (Claude), NA-24 (Gemini), NB-06 (ChatGPT API), F-19.
- SPECULATIVE: rules R-xx compress `do_this` lines; a rule resting on E3 evidence is a best-available default, not a tested tactic.
